### Worker deadline survives controller loss (2026-10-13)

The packaged worker previously supervised native commands with an infinite
internal deadline. A controller hard crash could therefore leave the original
worker running indefinitely even though its durable binding prevented duplicate
dispatch. New `run_command` requests use envelope v2 carrying the original
monotonic deadline. The worker validates it, refuses an already-expired request
before launching the native command, and passes it to the canonical process
supervisor. Timeout exits with code 124. The executor is explicitly local to the
daemon host; this deadline relies on their shared monotonic clock.

The saved binding retains the worker deadline. `get_command_result` reconstructs
the exact original envelope and verifies its existing payload hash; modifying
the deadline cannot reuse the result. Legacy bindings without this field retain
the v1 envelope interpretation. Existing pending work is never re-dispatched.
Generic raw `run` is still a host-controlled primitive; this new worker deadline
applies to packaged `run_command`, not arbitrary image entrypoints.

All 74 container/Codex tests passed, including standalone worker deadlines,
expired/malformed input, legacy recovery and changed-binding refusal. An initial
new-test placement error produced two NameErrors; corrected the test placement,
retaining the initial log and final report separately. The diagnostic wheel has
331 matching Python sources and SHA256 `eb51d28c24de0105b8ac5094492c573244119df969747ff80e93901183235d07`.
The remote image is `sha256:1340c8f32dafdb48e2296edd5b764a3651936cbed5ac4f0e32aaf9ed2a3dc1d0`.

On Personal-Agent, a real worker launched a 60-second fixture process with a
five-second deadline. After confirming startup, killed the Docker attach client
with SIGKILL and verified that the worker was still running. It then stopped
itself at its deadline with exit 124. No model call or login occurred. Evidence:
`worker-deadline-evidence.json`, focused test XML, and `worker-deadline-remote/`
under the completion output directory. The stopped worker is retained remotely.
These are focused artifacts, not the final paired release. Lost original-result
reconciliation, native authentication and application profile integration remain
open; bounding an orphan's lifetime does not settle its domain receipt.

and receive no invented historical IDs. Init resume preserves and validates the

amount calculations remain the canonical implementation.

### Original accounting policy and offline init cost reconciliation

New reservations bind `actual` or `fixed` accounting before dispatch in both the
budget journal and native trace context. The shared quality runner selects fixed
judge accounting; generation retains actual-cost accounting. Missing policy in
older entries remains unknown, so recovery cannot reinterpret the old charge.

Added `kb reconcile-init-cost --repo R --stage S --record ID --offline-confirmed`.
The application service requires a unique original successful native trace,
complete archive and matching reservation ID/mode/amount; the shared archive
validator now also cross-checks billing facts. `Budget.reconcile` performs the
single domain-owned settlement, replacing the old reservation's contribution
without allocating a new call. Known actual overruns remain recorded; unknown
actual cost retains the original conservative amount. Fixed accounting retains
its original allowance independently of native usage reporting. Settled receipts
are immutable, and repeated reconciliation is idempotent.

Discovery now writes `discovery-budget/2` as its financial authority; the init
record mirrors the total. Legacy journals first migrate from the conservative
maximum. New journal resume no longer reinstates a stale higher mirror after a
verified refund. A crash after journal settlement but before mirror save is
repaired by repeating the same command, without another charge or model call.
This resolves the overlapping total-authority issue encountered during recovery.

validator directly. Publication and final-head checks remain with their hosts.

fingerprint for installed packages as well as source checkouts. The neutral
fingerprint owner shares the traversal/serialization implementation; prior source
fingerprints preserve their bytes/order. Offline quality recovery refreshes the
fingerprint rather than trusting a process's old cache and requires the original
runtime identity. Failed fingerprint construction never caches a partial identity.
Legacy traces lacking the content fingerprint remain unreconciled instead of
being interpreted from a version string alone. Full native execution-profile and
credential parity is still a separate open gate.

## Framework-first handoff (2026-10-13)

ReviewRuntime reservation/start methods accepted only Strict input. They now
accept the neutral ReviewRunRequest union, dispatch Direct/Strict strategies
through the existing run service, and retain reserve_direct_review as a pure
delegating compatibility surface. No persisted input shape changed. Direct
integration checks all spellings reuse one run; a Strict SDK integration now
exercises the actual workflow and typed publication input.

The reproducible framework entry is tools/verify_capability_framework.py. Its
verified paired run completed successfully, with no production or test-source
drift and no skipped cases. It covers CLI/MCP/SDK entry, application composition,

## Original container execution is pending across preflight (2026-10-13)

Profile-wiring inspection found a duplicate-dispatch gap. A container with an
existing binding rejects dispatch before the current invocation sends anything.
The shared completion boundary previously translated that into a fallback-eligible
failure, and the journal could treat it as safe to retry despite the unresolved
original execution. The regression first reproduced both valid-shaped and corrupt
bindings reaching a replacement generator.

ContainerPending now marks retained original execution separately from this
invocation's sent/completed facts. The canonical unknown-outcome predicate honors
that marker. Completion translation and finalization failures preserve it;
fallback-summary and evolved-intake rollback adapters delegate their fact transfer
to budgeting.copy_native_outcome. Their two inline copy bodies are removed.
Current-call accounting remains sent=false and settles once; the original binding
and dispatch intent remain available for reconciliation.

Source validation covered primary/fallback paths, record/archive/settlement
failures, corrupt bindings, generic completion/accounting behavior, quality
journals, intake ledgers and ownership guards. The evolved-intake fixture initially
lacked Settings serialization; its corrected run and earlier failure are retained.
Sessions 68461, 13470 and 85370 exited 0. Evidence and the incremental patch against
the frozen installed framework candidate are under container-prior-execution-20261013.
Production delta is +17 lines / +114 AST nodes across five files. This is source
evidence for a later candidate, not a re-labeling of the prior wheel's results.

0.162.1 binary and successful ChatGPT login were verified through SSH (session
30558 exit 0); it is outside the noninteractive SSH PATH. No credentials were
printed and no model call ran. A dedicated-cache answer is no longer required.
Host login still does not attest container authentication or tool-free capability.
